AIBUILD RENDER 58x30x63 (x-4..53 y-6..23 z-9..53) states=213 symbols=2char
Coordinates: x -> east (columns), z -> south (rows), y -> up; front faces SOUTH (+z).
Each cell is a 2-char code (see LEGEND); .. = air; ,, = untouched terrain (unset ground y<=0, layers only). In elevation/top views a blank cell means no block along that ray. Read coordinates from the rulers: column = x (or z in east/west views), row = z (or y).
LEGEND - every cell is a 2-char code: 1st char = material, 2nd char = orientation.
  stairs:  ^ > v < = facing north/east/south/west (side of the TALL BACK; you walk up toward it)
           n e s w = upside-down (half=top) stairs facing north/east/south/west
  slabs:   _ = bottom half, ~ = top half, = = double;   logs/pillars: - axis x, / axis z, doubled = axis y
  other directional blocks (doors, trapdoors when open, torches, ladders, beds, chests...): ^ > v < = facing;
           closed trapdoors _ bottom / ~ top; u/d = facing up/down; h = hanging; doubled char = no orientation
  .. = air, ,, = untouched natural ground. Auto-computed props (stair shape, fence/pane/wall connections,
  waterlogged, leaves distance) are not shown.
